Copy totals for beginning/adds/amort columns, compute ending columns via rollforward\n    ws.cell(row=ending_balance_row, column=2, value=f\"=B{month_totals_row}\")\n\n    # Jan\n    ws.cell(row=ending_balance_row, column=3, value=f\"=C{month_totals_row}\")\n    ws.cell(row=ending_balance_row, column=4, value=f\"=D{month_totals_row}\")\n    ws.cell(row=ending_balance_row, column=5, value=f\"=B{ending_balance_row}+C{ending_balance_row}-D{ending_balance_row}\")\n\n    #
